        }
    }
}

// Error type - this would be defined elsewhere
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]

impl From<MzZipError> for i32 {
    fn from(err: MzZipError) -> i32 {
        match err {
            MzZipError::InvalidParameter => 1,
            MzZipError::InvalidState => 2,
            MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge => 3,
            MzZipError::FileTooLarge => 4,
            MzZipError::UnsupportedEncryption => 5,
            MzZipError::UnsupportedMethod => 6,
            MzZipError::UnsupportedFeature => 7,
            MzZipError::FileReadFailed => 8,
            MzZipError::FileOpenFailed => 9,
            MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted => 10,
            MzZipError::ValidationFailed => 11,
            MzZipError::AllocFailed => 12,
        }
    }
}

// Missing constants required by functions below
pub const MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ZIP64: u32 = 0x8000;
pub const MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING: u32 = 0x4000;
pub const MZ_ZIP_TYPE_USER: u32 = 0;
pub const MZ_ZIP_TYPE_HEAP: u32 = 1;
pub const MZ_ZIP_TYPE_FILE: u32 = 2;
pub const MZ_ZIP_MODE_INVALID: u32 = 0;
pub const MZ_ZIP_MODE_WRITING: u32 = 2;
pub const MZ_ZIP_MODE_WRITING_HAS_BEEN_FINALIZED: u32 = 3;

// Required error codes (assuming these exist in MzZipError)
// We'll reference them but MzZipError enum is defined elsewhere

impl MzZipArchive {
    /// Internal writer cleanup function
    pub fn writer_end_internal(&mut self, set_last_error: bool) -> bool {
        // Check for valid parameters
        if self.m_p_state.is_none() ||
           self.m_p_all.is_none() ||
           self.m_p_free.is_none() ||
           (self.m_zip_mode != MZ_ZIP_MODE_WRITING && 
            self.m_zip_mode != MZ_ZIP_MODE_WRITING_HAS_BEEN_FINALIZED)
        {
            if set_last_error {
                // mz_zip_set_error should be defined elsewhere
                // self.set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            }
            return false;
        }

        // Take ownership of the state
        let state = self.m_p_state.take().expect("State should exist");
        
        // Clear arrays
        state.m_central_dir.clear();
        state.m_central_dir_offsets.clear();
        state.m_sorted_central_dir_offsets.clear();

        // Handle file if present
        #[cfg(not(feature = "no_stdio"))]
        {
            if state.m_p_file.is_some() && self.m_zip_type == MZ_ZIP_TYPE_FILE {
                // File will be automatically closed when dropped
                // Just set to None
                if set_last_error {
                    // Check for close errors - in Rust we can't check fclose return
                    // We'll assume success since std::fs::File doesn't return errors on close
                }
            }
        }

        // Handle heap memory if present
        if self.m_p_write == Some(Self::heap_write_func) && !state.m_p_mem.is_empty() {
            // Memory will be freed when state is dropped (Vec<u8>)
        }

        // State is dropped here, freeing all resources
        self.m_zip_mode = MZ_ZIP_MODE_INVALID;
        true
    }

    /// Finalize writer
    pub fn writer_end(&mut self) -> bool {
        self.writer_end_internal(true)
    }

    /// Initialize writer with flags
    pub fn writer_init_v2(&mut self, existing_size: u64, flags: u32) -> bool {
        let zip64 = (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ZIP64) != 0;

        // Check for valid parameters
        if self.m_p_state.is_some() ||
           self.m_p_write.is_none() ||
           self.m_zip_mode != MZ_ZIP_MODE_INVALID
        {
            // self.set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            return false;
        }

        if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING) != 0 && self.m_p_read.is_none() {
            // self.set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            return false;
        }

        // Check file offset alignment is power of 2
        if self.m_file_offset_alignment != 0 && 
           (self.m_file_offset_alignment & (self.m_file_offset_alignment - 1)) != 0
        {
            // self.set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            return false;
        }

        // Set default allocators if not provided
        if self.m_p_all.is_none() {
            // Use default Rust allocator (Vec/Box)
        }
        if self.m_p_free.is_none() {
            // Use default Rust deallocator
        }
        if self.m_p_realloc.is_none() {
            // Use default Rust reallocator
        }

        self.m_archive_size = existing_size;
        self.m_central_directory_file_ofs = 0;
        self.m_total_files = 0;

        // Create internal state
        let state = Box::new(MzZipInternalState {
            m_central_dir: MzZipArray {
                m_p: Vec::new(),
                m_size: 0,
                m_capacity: 0,
                m_element_size: 1, // sizeof(u8)
            },
            m_central_dir_offsets: MzZipArray {
                m_p: Vec::new(),
                m_size: 0,
                m_capacity: 0,
                m_element_size: 4, // sizeof(u32)
            },
            m_sorted_central_dir_offsets: MzZipArray {
                m_p: Vec::new(),
                m_size: 0,
                m_capacity: 0,
                m_element_size: 4, // sizeof(u32)
            },
            m_init_flags: flags,
            m_zip64: zip64,
            m_zip64_has_extended_info_fields: zip64,
            #[cfg(not(feature = "no_stdio"))]
            m_p_file: None,
            m_file_archive_start_ofs: 0,
            m_p_mem: Vec::new(),
            m_mem_size: 0,
            m_mem_capacity: 0,
        });

        self.m_p_state = Some(state);
        self.m_zip_type = MZ_ZIP_TYPE_USER;
        self.m_zip_mode = MZ_ZIP_MODE_WRITING;

        true
    }

    /// Initialize writer (legacy version)
    pub fn writer_init(&mut self, existing_size: u64) -> bool {
        self.writer_init_v2(existing_size, 0)
    }

    /// Initialize heap writer with flags
    pub fn writer_init_heap_v2(
        &mut self,
        size_to_reserve_at_beginning: usize,
        initial_allocation_size: usize,
        flags: u32,
    ) -> bool {
        self.m_p_write = Some(Self::heap_write_func);
        // m_pNeeds_keepalive not in Rust types, ignoring
        
        if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING) != 0 {
            // self.m_p_read = Some(Self::mem_read_func);
            // mem_read_func not defined, would need to be implemented
        }

        self.m_p_io_opaque = Some(Box::new(())); // Simple placeholder

        if !self.writer_init_v2(size_to_reserve_at_beginning as u64, flags) {
            return false;
        }

        self.m_zip_type = MZ_ZIP_TYPE_HEAP;

        let actual_alloc_size = initial_allocation_size.max(size_to_reserve_at_beginning);
        if actual_alloc_size > 0 {
            if let Some(state) = &mut self.m_p_state {
                state.m_p_mem = vec![0u8; actual_alloc_size];
                state.m_mem_capacity = actual_alloc_size;
                state.m_mem_size = 0;
            } else {
                // Clean up on failure
                let _ = self.writer_end_internal(false);
                // self.set_error(MzZipError::AllocFailed);
                return false;
            }
        }

        true
    }

    /// Initialize heap writer (legacy version)
    pub fn writer_init_heap(
        &mut self,
        size_to_reserve_at_beginning: usize,
        initial_allocation_size: usize,
    ) -> bool {
        self.writer_init_heap_v2(size_to_reserve_at_beginning, initial_allocation_size, 0)
    }

    /// Initialize file writer (legacy version)
    pub fn writer_init_file(
        &mut self,
        filename: &str,
        size_to_reserve_at_beginning: u64,
    ) -> bool {
        self.writer_init_file_v2(filename, size_to_reserve_at_beginning, 0)
    }

    /// Initialize file writer with flags
    pub fn writer_init_file_v2(
        &mut self,
        filename: &str,
        size_to_reserve_at_beginning: u64,
        flags: u32,
    ) -> bool {
        #[cfg(feature = "no_stdio")]
        {
            // self.set_error(MzZipError::FileOpenFailed);
            return false;
        }

        #[cfg(not(feature = "no_stdio"))]
        {
            self.m_p_write = Some(Self::file_write_func);
            // m_pNeeds_keepalive not in Rust types
            
            if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING) != 0 {
                // self.m_p_read = Some(Self::file_read_func);
                // file_read_func not defined
            }

            self.m_p_io_opaque = Some(Box::new(())); // Placeholder

            if !self.writer_init_v2(size_to_reserve_at_beginning, flags) {
                return false;
            }

            // Open the file
            let mode = if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING) != 0 {
                "w+b"
            } else {
                "wb"
            };

            let file = match std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
                .read((flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING) != 0)
                .write(true)
                .create(true)
                .truncate(true)
                .open(filename)
            {
                Ok(f) => f,
                Err(_) => {
                    let _ = self.writer_end();
                    // self.set_error(MzZipError::FileOpenFailed);
                    return false;
                }
            };

            if let Some(state) = &mut self.m_p_state {
                state.m_p_file = Some(file);
            }

            self.m_zip_type = MZ_ZIP_TYPE_FILE;

            // Reserve space at beginning if requested
            if size_to_reserve_at_beginning > 0 {
                let mut cur_ofs = 0u64;
                let mut remaining = size_to_reserve_at_beginning;
                let mut buf = [0u8; 4096];

                while remaining > 0 {
                    let n = (buf.len() as u64).min(remaining) as usize;
                    if let Err(_) = self.file_write_func(cur_ofs, &buf[..n]) {
                        let _ = self.writer_end();
                        // self.set_error(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                        return false;
                    }
                    cur_ofs += n as u64;
                    remaining -= n as u64;
                }
            }

            true
        }
    }

    /// File write function callback
    fn file_write_func(&mut self, file_ofs: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> Result<usize, ()> {
        #[cfg(not(feature = "no_stdio"))]
        {
            if let Some(state) = &mut self.m_p_state {
                if let Some(file) = &mut state.m_p_file {
                    let target_ofs = file_ofs + state.m_file_archive_start_ofs;
                    
                    // Seek to position
                    if let Err(_) = file.seek(std::io::SeekFrom::Start(target_ofs)) {
                        // self.set_error(MzZipError::FileSeekFailed);
                        return Err(());
                    }
                    
                    // Write data
                    match file.write_all(buf) {
                        Ok(_) => Ok(buf.len()),
                        Err(_) => {
                            // self.set_error(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                            Err(())
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    // self.set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
                    Err(())
                }
            } else {
                // self.set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
                Err(())
            }
        }
        #[cfg(feature = "no_stdio")]
        {
            // self.set_error(MzZipError::NotSupported);
            Err(())
        }
    }

    /// Heap write function (placeholder - would need actual implementation)
    fn heap_write_func(&mut self, _file_ofs: u64, _buf: &[u8]) -> Result<usize, ()> {
        // Implementation would write to m_p_mem buffer
        // self.set_error(MzZipError::NotImplemented);
        Err(())
    }
}

// Little-endian write helpers



// --- Module: mz_p6 ---
// Constants from miniz.h

// Local directory header offsets

// Helper function to write little-endian values



// Helper for min/max
const fn mz_min(a: u32, b: u32) -> u32 {
    if a < b { a } else { b }
}

// Error type
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]

// Define MzZipArchive struct

struct MzZipState {
    m_zip64: bool,
    m_p_file: Option<std::fs::File>,
    m_p_mem: Vec<u8>,
    m_mem_capacity: usize,
    m_mem_size: usize,
    m_sorted_central_dir_offsets: Vec<u64>,
}

impl MzZipArchive {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            m_p_state: None,
            m_zip_mode: 0,
            m_total_files: 0,
            m_archive_size: 0,
            m_central_directory_file_ofs: 0,
            m_zip_type: 0,
            m_p_all_opaque: None,
        }
    }

    pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_cfile(&mut self, p_file: Option<std::fs::File>, flags: u32) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter)
    }

    pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_from_reader_v2(&mut self, p_filename: Option<&str>, flags: u32) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        let state = self.m_p_state.as_mut().ok_or(MzZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
        
        if self.m_zip_mode != 1 {
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }

        if (flags & 0x1000) != 0 {
            if !state.m_zip64 {
                return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            }
        }

        if state.m_zip64 {
            if self.m_total_files == u32::MAX {
                return Err(MzZipError::TooManyFiles);
            }
        } else {
            if self.m_total_files == 0xFFFF {
                return Err(MzZipError::TooManyFiles);
            }
            
            if (self.m_archive_size + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE) > u32::MAX as u64 {
                return Err(MzZipError::FileTooLarge);
            }
        }

        if state.m_p_file.is_some() {
            if p_filename.is_none() && (self.m_zip_type == 1) {
                return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            }
        } else if state.m_p_mem.is_empty() {
            if self.m_p_all_opaque.is_none() {
                return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            }
            state.m_mem_capacity = state.m_mem_size;
        }

        self.m_archive_size = self.m_central_directory_file_ofs;
        self.m_central_directory_file_ofs = 0;

        state.m_sorted_central_dir_offsets.clear();

        self.m_zip_mode = 2;
        
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_from_reader(&mut self, p_filename: Option<&str>) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        self.mz_zip_writer_init_from_reader_v2(p_filename, 0)
    }

    pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_mem(&mut self, archive_name: &str, buf: &[u8], level_and_flags: u32) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        self.mz_zip_writer_add_mem_ex(
            archive_name,
            buf,
            None,
            0,
            level_and_flags,
            0,
            0
        )
    }

    fn write_data(&self, offset: u64, data: &[u8]) -> Result<usize, MzZipError> {
        Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed)
    }

    fn mz_zip_writer_compute_padding_needed_for_file_alignment(&self) -> u32 {
        0
    }

    fn mz_zip_writer_write_zeros(&mut self, cur_file_ofs: u64, n: u32) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        const BUF_SIZE: usize = 4096;
        let mut buf = [0u8; BUF_SIZE];
        
        let mut remaining = n;
        let mut offset = cur_file_ofs;
        
        while remaining > 0 {
            let to_write = std::cmp::min(BUF_SIZE as u32, remaining) as usize;
            let written = self.write_data(offset, &buf[..to_write])?;
            
            if written != to_write {
                return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
            }
            
            offset += written as u64;
            remaining -= written as u32;
        }
        
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_mem_ex(
        &mut self,
        archive_name: &str,
        p_buf: &[u8],
        p_comment: Option<&[u8]>,
        comment_size: u16,
        level_and_flags: u32,
        uncomp_size: u64,
        uncomp_crc32: u32,
    ) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter)
    }
}

// Callback state for compression

fn mz_zip_writer_add_put_buf_callback(p_buf: &[u8], p_user: &mut MzZipWriterAddState) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
    let written = p_user.p_zip.write_data(p_user.cur_archive_file_ofs, p_buf)?;
    if written != p_buf.len() {
        return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
    }
    
    p_user.cur_archive_file_ofs += written as u64;
    p_user.comp_size += written as u64;
    Ok(())
}

fn mz_zip_writer_create_zip64_extra_data(
    p_buf: &mut [u8],
    p_uncomp_size: Option<&u64>,
    p_comp_size: Option<&u64>,
    p_local_header_ofs: Option<&u64>,
) -> u32 {
    let mut p_dst = 0;
    let mut field_size = 0;

    mz_write_le16(&mut p_buf[p_dst..], MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID);
    p_dst += 2;
    
    p_dst += 2;

    if let Some(size) = p_uncomp_size {
        mz_write_le64(&mut p_buf[p_dst..], *size);
        p_dst += 8;
        field_size += 8;
    }

    if let Some(size) = p_comp_size {
        mz_write_le64(&mut p_buf[p_dst..], *size);
        p_dst += 8;
        field_size += 8;
    }

    if let Some(ofs) = p_local_header_ofs {
        mz_write_le64(&mut p_buf[p_dst..], *ofs);
        p_dst += 8;
        field_size += 8;
    }

    mz_write_le16(&mut p_buf[2..4], field_size as u16);

    p_dst as u32
}

fn mz_zip_writer_create_local_dir_header(
    p_dst: &mut [u8],
    filename_size: u16,
    extra_size: u16,
    uncomp_size: u64,
    comp_size: u64,
    uncomp_crc32: u32,
    method: u16,
    bit_flags: u16,
    dos_time: u16,
    dos_date: u16,
) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
    if p_dst.len() < MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as usize {
        return Err(MzZipError::InternalError);
    }

    p_dst[..MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as usize].fill(0);

    mz_write_le32(&mut p_dst[MZ_ZIP_LDH_SIG_OFS..], MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG);
    mz_write_le16(&mut p_dst[MZ_ZIP_LDH_VERSION_NEEDED_OFS..], if method != 0 { 20 } else { 0 });
    mz_write_le16(&mut p_dst[MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS..], bit_flags);
    mz_write_le16(&mut p_dst[MZ_ZIP_LDH_METHOD_OFS..], method);
    mz_write_le16(&mut p_dst[MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILE_TIME_OFS..], dos_time);
    mz_write_le16(&mut p_dst[MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILE_DATE_OFS..], dos_date);
    mz_write_le32(&mut p_dst[MZ_ZIP_LDH_CRC32_OFS..], uncomp_crc32);
    mz_write_le32(&mut p_dst[MZ_ZIP_LDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..], mz_min(comp_size as u32, u32::MAX));
    mz_write_le32(&mut p_dst[MZ_ZIP_LDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..], mz_min(uncomp_size as u32, u32::MAX));
    mz_write_le16(&mut p_dst[MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..], filename_size);
    mz_write_le16(&mut p_dst[MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..], extra_size);

    Ok(())
}

fn mz_zip_writer_validate_archive_name(p_archive_name: &str) -> bool {
    if p_archive_name.starts_with('/') {
        return false;
    }
    
    true
}

impl MzZipArray {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { data: Vec::new() }
    }
    
    pub fn reserve(&mut self, capacity: usize) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        self.data.try_reserve(capacity).map_err(|_| MzZipError::AllocFailed)
    }
    
    pub fn clear(&mut self) {
        self.data.clear();
    }
    
    pub fn push(&mut self, byte: u8) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        self.data.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| MzZipError::AllocFailed)?;
        self.data.push(byte);
        Ok(())
    }
}

// Now implement the actual functions from the C source

/// Adds a file to a zip archive using a read callback function
/// This is a complex function that handles zip64 extensions and compression
pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_read_buf_callback(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    p_archive_name: &str,
    read_callback: MzFileReadFunc,
    callback_opaque: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    max_size: u64,
    p_file_time: Option<&MzTimeT>,
    p_comment: Option<&[u8]>,
    comment_size: u16,
    level_and_flags: u32,
    user_extra_data: Option<&[u8]>,
    user_extra_data_len: u32,
    user_extra_data_central: Option<&[u8]>,
    user_extra_data_central_len: u32,
) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
    // Extract level from flags
    let mut level = level_and_flags & 0xF;
    if (level_and_flags as i32) < 0 {
        level = MZ_DEFAULT_LEVEL;
    }
    
    // Generate flags
    let mut gen_flags = if (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_HEADER_SET_SIZE) == 0 {
        MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_HAS_LOCATOR
    } else {
        0
    };
    
    if (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_ASCII_FILENAME) == 0 {
        gen_flags |= MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_UTF8;
    }
    
    // Sanity checks
    if p_zip.m_zip_mode != MzZipMode::Writing {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    if p_archive_name.is_empty() {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    if comment_size > 0 && p_comment.is_none() {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    if level > MZ_UBER_COMPRESSION {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    let p_state = p_zip.m_pstate.as_mut().ok_or(MzZipError::InternalError)?;
    
    // Check for zip64 requirements
    if !p_state.zip64 && max_size > MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 {
        p_state.zip64 = true;
    }
    
    // We don't support compressed data flag
    if (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) != 0 {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    // Validate archive name
    if !mz_zip_writer_validate_archive_name(p_archive_name) {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    // Check file count limits
    if p_state.zip64 {
        if p_zip.m_total_files == MZ_UINT32_MAX {
            return Err(MzZipError::TooManyFiles);
        }
    } else if p_zip.m_total_files == MZ_UINT16_MAX as u32 {
        p_state.zip64 = true;
    }
    
    let archive_name_size = p_archive_name.len();
    if archive_name_size > MZ_UINT16_MAX as usize {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    let num_alignment_padding_bytes = mz_zip_writer_compute_padding_needed_for_file_alignment(p_zip);
    
    // Convert time to DOS format if provided
    let (dos_time, dos_date) = if let Some(file_time) = p_file_time {
        let mut dos_time = 0u16;
        let mut dos_date = 0u16;
        mz_zip_time_t_to_dos_time(*file_time, &mut dos_time, &mut dos_date);
        (dos_time, dos_date)
    } else {
        (0u16, 0u16)
    };
    
    // Handle zero compression level for very small files
    if max_size <= 3 {
        level = 0;
    }
    
    // Write alignment padding
    mz_zip_writer_write_zeros(p_zip, p_zip.m_archive_size, num_alignment_padding_bytes)?;
    
    let mut cur_archive_file_ofs = p_zip.m_archive_size + num_alignment_padding_bytes as u64;
    let local_dir_header_ofs = cur_archive_file_ofs;
    
    // Determine compression method
    let method = if max_size > 0 && level > 0 {
        MZ_DEFLATED as u16
    } else {
        0
    };
    
    // Handle zip64 vs non-zip64 paths
    let mut extra_data = [0u8; MZ_ZIP64_MAX_CENTRAL_EXTRA_FIELD_SIZE as usize];
    let mut extra_size = 0u32;
    let mut p_extra_data: Option<&[u8]> = None;
    
    if p_state.zip64 {
        if max_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 || local_dir_header_ofs >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 {
            p_extra_data = Some(&extra_data);
            // Simplified - in real code this would populate the extra_data buffer
            extra_size = 0;
        }
        
        // Write local directory header
        // This is simplified - actual implementation would write the header
    } else {
        // Non-zip64 path
        if cur_archive_file_ofs > MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 {
            return Err(MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
        }
    }
    
    // Write the file data with or without compression
    let (uncomp_size, comp_size, uncomp_crc32) = if max_size > 0 {
        if level == 0 {
            // Store without compression
            let mut file_ofs = 0u64;
            let mut uncomp_crc32 = MZ_CRC32_INIT as u32;
            let read_buf_size = MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE;
            
            // Allocate read buffer using the zip's allocator
            let mut read_buf = vec![0u8; read_buf_size];
            
            loop {
                let n = read_callback(callback_opaque.clone(), file_ofs, &mut read_buf);
                if n == 0 {
                    break;
                }
                
                if n > read_buf_size || file_ofs + n as u64 > max_size {
                    return Err(MzZipError::FileReadFailed);
                }
                
                // Write to archive
                // In real implementation: p_zip.m_pwrite would be called
                
                file_ofs += n as u64;
                // Update CRC32
                uncomp_crc32 = mz_crc32(uncomp_crc32 as u64, &read_buf[..n]) as u32;
                cur_archive_file_ofs += n as u64;
            }
            
            (file_ofs, file_ofs, uncomp_crc32)
        } else {
            // Compress the data
            // This is simplified - actual implementation would use tdefl compressor
            (0u64, 0u64, 0u32)
        }
    } else {
        (0u64, 0u64, 0u32)
    };
    
    // Write data descriptor if needed
    if (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_HEADER_SET_SIZE) == 0 {
        // Write data descriptor
        // This is simplified
    }
    
    // Update central directory
    mz_zip_writer_add_to_central_dir(
        p_zip,
        p_archive_name,
        archive_name_size as u16,
        p_extra_data.unwrap_or(&[]),
        extra_size as u16,
        p_comment,
        comment_size,
        uncomp_size,
        comp_size,
        uncomp_crc32,
        method,
        gen_flags,
        dos_time,
        dos_date,
        local_dir_header_ofs,
        0, // ext_attributes
        user_extra_data_central,
        user_extra_data_central_len,
    )?;
    
    p_zip.m_total_files += 1;
    p_zip.m_archive_size = cur_archive_file_ofs;
    
    Ok(())
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]

// Helper functions for reading/writing little-endian values
fn read_le_u16(data: &[u8], offset: usize) -> u16 {
    u16::from_le_bytes([data[offset], data[offset + 1]])
}

fn read_le_u32(data: &[u8], offset: usize) -> u32 {
    u32::from_le_bytes([
        data[offset],
        data[offset + 1],
        data[offset + 2],
        data[offset + 3],
    ])
}

fn write_le_u16(data: &mut [u8], offset: usize, value: u16) {
    let bytes = value.to_le_bytes();
    data[offset..offset + 2].copy_from_slice(&bytes);
}

fn write_le_u32(data: &mut [u8], offset: usize, value: u32) {
    let bytes = value.to_le_bytes();
    data[offset..offset + 4].copy_from_slice(&bytes);
}

fn write_le_u64(data: &mut [u8], offset: usize, value: u64) {
    let bytes = value.to_le_bytes();
    data[offset..offset + 8].copy_from_slice(&bytes);
}

// Rust equivalents of C structures

impl MzZipArray {
    pub fn new(element_size: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            data: Vec::new(),
            element_size,
        }
    }

    pub fn clear(&mut self) {
        self.data.clear();
    }

    pub fn size(&self) -> usize {
        self.data.len() / self.element_size
    }

    pub fn push_back<T>(&mut self, item: &T) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        let bytes = unsafe {
            slice::from_raw_parts(
                item as *const T as *const u8,
                mem::size_of::<T>(),
            )
        };
        if mem::size_of::<T>() != self.element_size {
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }
        self.data.extend_from_slice(bytes);
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn as_slice<T>(&self) -> &[T] {
        unsafe {
            slice::from_raw_parts(
                self.data.as_ptr() as *const T,
                self.data.len() / mem::size_of::<T>(),
            )
        }
    }

    pub fn resize(&mut self, new_size: usize, preserve_data: bool) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        let new_len = new_size * self.element_size;
        if preserve_data {
            self.data.resize(new_len, 0);
        } else {
            self.data = vec![0; new_len];
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[cfg(windows)]

// I/O callback traits
pub trait ZipIoCallbacks {
    fn read(&mut self, file_ofs: u64, buf: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, MzZipError>;
    fn write(&mut self, file_ofs: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> Result<usize, MzZipError>;
    fn tell(&self) -> Result<u64, MzZipError>;
    fn seek(&mut self, offset: i64, whence: i32) -> Result<(), MzZipError>;
}

// File-based I/O implementation
pub struct FileIo {
    file: File,
}

impl FileIo {
    pub fn new(filename: &str) -> Result<Self, MzZipError> {
        let file = OpenOptions::new()
            .read(true)
            .write(true)
            .create(true)
            .open(filename)
            .map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileReadFailed)?;
        Ok(Self { file })
    }
}

impl ZipIoCallbacks for FileIo {
    fn read(&mut self, file_ofs: u64, buf: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, MzZipError> {
        self.file
            .seek(SeekFrom::Start(file_ofs))
            .map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileReadFailed)?;
        self.file
            .read(buf)
            .map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileReadFailed)
    }

    fn write(&mut self, file_ofs: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> Result<usize, MzZipError> {
        self.file
            .seek(SeekFrom::Start(file_ofs))
            .map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileWriteFailed)?;
        self.file
            .write(buf)
            .map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileWriteFailed)
    }

    fn tell(&self) -> Result<u64, MzZipError> {
        self.file
            .stream_position()
            .map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileReadFailed)
    }

    fn seek(&mut self, offset: i64, whence: i32) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        let pos = match whence {
            0 => SeekFrom::Start(offset as u64),
            1 => SeekFrom::Current(offset),
            2 => SeekFrom::End(offset),
            _ => return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter),
        };
        self.file
            .seek(pos)
            .map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileReadFailed)?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

// Memory-based I/O implementation
pub struct MemIo {
    data: Vec<u8>,
    position: usize,
}

impl MemIo {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            data: Vec::new(),
            position: 0,
        }
    }

    pub fn with_capacity(capacity: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            data: Vec::with_capacity(capacity),
            position: 0,
        }
    }

    pub fn into_inner(self) -> Vec<u8> {
        self.data
    }
}

impl ZipIoCallbacks for MemIo {
    fn read(&mut self, file_ofs: u64, buf: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, MzZipError> {
        let ofs = file_ofs as usize;
        if ofs >= self.data.len() {
            return Ok(0);
        }
        let available = self.data.len() - ofs;
        let to_read = buf.len().min(available);
        buf[..to_read].copy_from_slice(&self.data[ofs..ofs + to_read]);
        Ok(to_read)
    }

    fn write(&mut self, file_ofs: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> Result<usize, MzZipError> {
        let ofs = file_ofs as usize;
        if ofs + buf.len() > self.data.len() {
            self.data.resize(ofs + buf.len(), 0);
        }
        self.data[ofs..ofs + buf.len()].copy_from_slice(buf);
        Ok(buf.len())
    }

    fn tell(&self) -> Result<u64, MzZipError> {
        Ok(self.position as u64)
    }

    fn seek(&mut self, offset: i64, whence: i32) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        match whence {
            0 => self.position = offset as usize,
            1 => self.position = (self.position as i64 + offset) as usize,
            2 => self.position = (self.data.len() as i64 + offset) as usize,
            _ => return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter),
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

// Main zip archive structure


#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ZipMode {
    Invalid,
    Reading,
    Writing,
    WritingHasBeenFinalized,
}


impl MzZipArchive {
    pub fn set_error(&mut self, error: MzZipError) -> bool {
        self.m_last_error = error;
        false
    }

    pub fn writer_compute_padding_needed_for_file_alignment(&self) -> u32 {
        if self.m_file_offset_alignment == 0 {
            return 0;
        }
        let padding = self.m_file_offset_alignment - (self.archive_size % self.m_file_offset_alignment as u64) as u32;
        if padding == self.m_file_offset_alignment {
            0
        } else {
            padding
        }
    }

    pub fn writer_write_zeros(&mut self, file_ofs: u64, num_bytes: u32) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        if num_bytes == 0 {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let zeros = vec![0u8; num_bytes as usize];
        self.m_p_io.write(file_ofs, &zeros)?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn writer_update_zip64_extension_block(
        &mut self,
        new_ext_block: &mut MzZipArray,
        p_src_ext: &[u8],
        src_ext_len: u32,
        comp_size: &u64,
        uncomp_size: &u64,
        local_header_ofs: &u64,
        disk_start: Option<&u32>,
    ) -> Result<bool, MzZipError> {
        // Simplified implementation - in reality would parse and update zip64 fields
        // For now, just copy the original extension block
        if src_ext_len > 0 {
            let slice = unsafe { slice::from_raw_parts(p_src_ext.as_ptr(), src_ext_len as usize) };
            new_ext_block.data.extend_from_slice(slice);
        }
        Ok(true)
    }
}

// Main translation of mz_zip_writer_add_from_zip_reader
pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_from_zip_reader(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    p_source_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    src_file_index: u32,
) -> Result<bool, MzZipError> {
    // Sanity checks
    if p_zip.m_zip_mode != ZipMode::Writing {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    let p_state = p_zip.m_p_state.as_mut().ok_or(MzZipError::InvalidParameter)?;

    // Don't support copying files from zip64 archives to non-zip64
    let source_state = p_source_zip.m_p_state.as_ref().ok_or(MzZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
    if source_state.m_zip64 && !p_state.m_zip64 {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    // Get source central directory header
    let src_cdh = get_central_dir_header(p_source_zip, src_file_index)
        .ok_or(MzZipError::InvalidParameter)?;

    // Verify signature
    if read_le_u32(&src_cdh, MZ_ZIP_CDH_SIG_OFS) != MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }

    let src_filename_len = read_le_u16(&src_cdh, MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS) as u32;
    let src_comment_len = read_le_u16(&src_cdh, MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS) as u32;
    let src_ext_len = read_le_u16(&src_cdh, MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS) as u32;
    let src_central_dir_following_data_size = src_filename_len + src_ext_len + src_comment_len;

    // Check central directory size limits
    if (p_state.m_central_dir.size() as u32 + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u32 +
        src_central_dir_following_data_size + 32) >= u32::MAX {
        return Err(MzZipError::UnsupportedCdirSize);
    }

    let num_alignment_padding_bytes = p_zip.writer_compute_padding_needed_for_file_alignment();

    // Check file count limits
    if !p_state.m_zip64 {
        if p_zip.total_files == u16::MAX as u32 {
            return Err(MzZipError::TooManyFiles);
        }
    } else {
        if p_zip.total_files == u32::MAX {
            return Err(MzZipError::TooManyFiles);
        }
    }

    // Get file stat
    let src_file_stat = mz_zip_file_stat_internal(p_source_zip, src_file_index, &src_cdh)
        .ok_or(MzZipError::InvalidParameter)?;

    let mut cur_src_file_ofs = src_file_stat.m_local_header_ofs;
    let mut cur_dst_file_ofs = p_zip.archive_size;

    // Read local directory header
    let mut local_header = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
    p_source_zip.m_p_io.read(cur_src_file_ofs, &mut local_header)?;
    cur_src_file_ofs += MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;

    if read_le_u32(&local_header, MZ_ZIP_LDH_SIG_OFS) != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }

    // Compute total size to copy
    let local_header_filename_size = read_le_u16(&local_header, MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS) as u64;
    let local_header_extra_len = read_le_u16(&local_header, MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS) as u64;
    let mut local_header_comp_size = read_le_u32(&local_header, MZ_ZIP_LDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS) as u64;
    let mut local_header_uncomp_size = read_le_u32(&local_header, MZ_ZIP_LDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS) as u64;
    let mut src_archive_bytes_remaining = src_file_stat.m_comp_size + local_header_filename_size + local_header_extra_len;

    // Check for zip64 extended information
    let mut found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir = false;
    if local_header_extra_len > 0 && (local_header_comp_size == u32::MAX as u64 || local_header_uncomp_size == u32::MAX as u64) {
        // Parse extra fields to find zip64 data
        let mut extra_data = vec![0u8; local_header_extra_len as usize];
        p_source_zip.m_p_io.read(
            src_file_stat.m_local_header_ofs + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 + local_header_filename_size,
            &mut extra_data,
        )?;

        let mut offset = 0;
        while offset + 4 <= extra_data.len() {
            let field_id = read_le_u16(&extra_data, offset);
            let field_data_size = read_le_u16(&extra_data, offset + 2) as usize;
            let field_total_size = field_data_size + 4;

            if offset + field_total_size > extra_data.len() {
                return Err(MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
            }

            if field_id == MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID {
                if field_data_size >= 16 {
                    let data_offset = offset + 4;
                    local_header_uncomp_size = read_le_u64_from_slice(&extra_data, data_offset);
                    local_header_comp_size = read_le_u64_from_slice(&extra_data, data_offset + 8);
                    found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir = true;
                }
                break;
            }

            offset += field_total_size;
        }
    }

    // Check archive size for non-zip64
    if !p_state.m_zip64 {
        let approx_new_archive_size = cur_dst_file_ofs + num_alignment_padding_bytes as u64 +
            MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 + src_archive_bytes_remaining + 16 +
            p_state.m_central_dir.size() as u64 + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 +
            src_central_dir_following_data_size as u64 + MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 + 64;

        if approx_new_archive_size >= u32::MAX as u64 {
            return Err(MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
        }
    }

    // Write padding
    p_zip.writer_write_zeros(cur_dst_file_ofs, num_alignment_padding_bytes)?;
    cur_dst_file_ofs += num_alignment_padding_bytes as u64;

    let local_dir_header_ofs = cur_dst_file_ofs;

    // Copy local header
    p_zip.m_p_io.write(cur_dst_file_ofs, &local_header)?;
    cur_dst_file_ofs += MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;

    // Copy file data
    let buf_size = 65536.min(src_archive_bytes_remaining as usize);
    let mut buffer = vec![0u8; buf_size];

    while src_archive_bytes_remaining > 0 {
        let to_read = buf_size.min(src_archive_bytes_remaining as usize);
        let n = p_source_zip.m_p_io.read(cur_src_file_ofs, &mut buffer[..to_read])?;
        cur_src_file_ofs += n as u64;

        p_zip.m_p_io.write(cur_dst_file_ofs, &buffer[..n])?;
        cur_dst_file_ofs += n as u64;

        src_archive_bytes_remaining -= n as u64;
    }

    // Handle data descriptor if present
    let bit_flags = read_le_u16(&local_header, MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS);
    if bit_flags & 8 != 0 {
        // Read and copy data descriptor
        let mut descriptor_buf = [0u8; 24]; // Max size for zip64 data descriptor
        let n = if source_state.m_zip64 || found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir {
            // Read 24 bytes (6*u32) for zip64
            p_source_zip.m_p_io.read(cur_src_file_ofs, &mut descriptor_buf[..24])?;
            if read_le_u32(&descriptor_buf, 0) == MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID {
                24
            } else {
                20
            }
        } else {
            // Read 16 bytes (4*u32) for non-zip64
            p_source_zip.m_p_io.read(cur_src_file_ofs, &mut descriptor_buf[..16])?;
            if read_le_u32(&descriptor_buf, 0) == MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID {
                16
            } else {
                12
            }
        };

        p_zip.m_p_io.write(cur_dst_file_ofs, &descriptor_buf[..n])?;
        cur_src_file_ofs += n as u64;
        cur_dst_file_ofs += n as u64;
    }

    // Add central directory header
    let orig_central_dir_size = p_state.m_central_dir.size();
    let mut new_central_header = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
    new_central_header.copy_from_slice(&src_cdh[..MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE]);

    if p_state.m_zip64 {
        // Update for zip64
        write_le_u32(&mut new_central_header, MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS, u32::MAX);
        write_le_u32(&mut new_central_header, MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS, u32::MAX);
        write_le_u32(&mut new_central_header, MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS, u32::MAX);

        let mut new_ext_block = MzZipArray::new(1);
        let src_ext_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_filename_len as usize;
        let src_ext = &src_cdh[src_ext_start..src_ext_start + src_ext_len as usize];

        if !p_zip.writer_update_zip64_extension_block(
            &mut new_ext_block,
            src_ext,
            src_ext_len,
            &src_file_stat.m_comp_size,
            &src_file_stat.m_uncomp_size,
            &local_dir_header_ofs,
            None,
        )? {
            return Ok(false);
        }

        write_le_u16(&mut new_central_header, MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS, new_ext_block.size() as u16);

        // Push updated central directory entry
        p_state.m_central_dir.push_back(&new_central_header)?;
        p_state.m_central_dir.data.extend_from_slice(&src_cdh[MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE..MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_filename_len as usize]);
        p_state.m_central_dir.data.extend_from_slice(&new_ext_block.data);
        p_state.m_central_dir.data.extend_from_slice(&src_cdh[MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_filename_len as usize + src_ext_len as usize..MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_filename_len as usize + src_ext_len as usize + src_comment_len as usize]);
    } else {
        // Non-zip64 version
        if cur_dst_file_ofs > u32::MAX as u64 {
            return Err(MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
        }
        if local_dir_header_ofs >= u32::MAX as u64 {
            return Err(MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
        }

        write_le_u32(&mut new_central_header, MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS, local_dir_header_ofs as u32);

        p_state.m_central_dir.push_back(&new_central_header)?;
        p_state.m_central_dir.data.extend_from_slice(&src_cdh[MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE..MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_central_dir_following_data_size as usize]);
    }

    // Update central directory offsets
    let n = orig_central_dir_size as u32;
    p_state.m_central_dir_offsets.push_back(&n)?;

    p_zip.total_files += 1;
    p_zip.archive_size = cur_dst_file_ofs;

    Ok(true)
}

// Helper function to read u64 from slice
fn read_le_u64_from_slice(data: &[u8], offset: usize) -> u64 {
    u64::from_le_bytes([
        data[offset],
        data[offset + 1],
        data[offset + 2],
        data[offset + 3],
        data[offset + 4],
        data[offset + 5],
        data[offset + 6],
        data[offset + 7],
    ])
}

// Stub implementations for missing functions
fn get_central_dir_header(_zip: &MzZipArchive, _index: u32) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
    // Implementation would read central directory header
    None
}


// Translation of mz_zip_writer_finalize_archive
pub fn mz_zip_writer_finalize_archive(p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive) -> Result<bool, MzZipError> {
    let p_state = p_zip.m_p_state.as_mut().ok_or(MzZipError::InvalidParameter)?;

    if p_zip.m_zip_mode != ZipMode::Writing {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    // Check limits
    if p_state.m_zip64 {
        if p_state.m_central_dir.size() >= u32::MAX as usize {
            return Err(MzZipError::TooManyFiles);
        }
    } else {
        if p_zip.total_files > u16::MAX as u32 ||
           (p_zip.archive_size + p_state.m_central_dir.size() as u64 + MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64) > u32::MAX as u64 {
            return Err(MzZipError::TooManyFiles);
        }
    }

    let mut central_dir_ofs = 0u64;
    let mut central_dir_size = 0u64;

    if p_zip.total_files > 0 {
        // Write central directory
        central_dir_ofs = p_zip.archive_size;
        central_dir_size = p_state.m_central_dir.size() as u64;
        p_zip.central_directory_file_ofs = central_dir_ofs;

        p_zip.m_p_io.write(central_dir_ofs, &p_state.m_central_dir.data)?;
        p_zip.archive_size += central_dir_size;
    }

    if p_state.m_zip64 {
        // Write zip64 end of central directory header
        let rel_ofs_to_zip64_ecdr = p_zip.archive_size;
        let mut hdr = [0u8; MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];

        write_le_u32(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_SIG_OFS, MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG);
        write_le_u64(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_SIZE_OF_RECORD_OFS, (MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE - 12) as u64);
        write_le_u16(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_VERSION_MADE_BY_OFS, 0x031E);
        write_le_u16(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_VERSION_NEEDED_OFS, 0x002D);
        write_le_u64(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_NUM_ENTRIES_ON_DISK_OFS, p_zip.total_files as u64);
        write_le_u64(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS, p_zip.total_files as u64);
        write_le_u64(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS, central_dir_size);
        write_le_u64(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS, central_dir_ofs);

        p_zip.m_p_io.write(p_zip.archive_size, &hdr)?;
        p_zip.archive_size += MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;

        // Write zip64 end of central directory locator
        let mut locator = [0u8; MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE];
        write_le_u32(&mut locator, MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_SIG_OFS, MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIG);
        write_le_u64(&mut locator, MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_REL_OFS_TO_ZIP64_ECDR_OFS, rel_ofs_to_zip64_ecdr);
        write_le_u32(&mut locator, MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_TOTAL_NUMBER_OF_DISKS_OFS, 1);

        p_zip.m_p_io.write(p_zip.archive_size, &locator)?;
        p_zip.archive_size += MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE as u64;
    }

    // Write end of central directory record
    let mut eocd = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
    write_le_u32(&mut eocd, MZ_ZIP_ECDH_SIG_OFS, MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG);
    
    let total_files_16 = p_zip.total_files.min(u16::MAX as u32) as u16;
    write_le_u16(&mut eocd, MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_NUM_ENTRIES_ON_DISK_OFS, total_files_16);
    write_le_u16(&mut eocd, MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS, total_files_16);
    
    let central_dir_size_32 = central_dir_size.min(u32::MAX as u64) as u32;
    write_le_u32(&mut eocd, MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS, central_dir_size_32);
    
    let central_dir_ofs_32 = central_dir_ofs.min(u32::MAX as u64) as u32;
    write_le_u32(&mut eocd, MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS, central_dir_ofs_32);

    p_zip.m_p_io.write(p_zip.archive_size, &eocd)?;
    p_zip.archive_size += MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;

    p_zip.m_zip_mode = ZipMode::WritingHasBeenFinalized;
    Ok(true)
}

// Translation of mz_zip_writer_finalize_heap_archive
pub fn mz_zip_writer_finalize_heap_archive(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    pp_buf: &mut Option<Vec<u8>>,
    p_size: &mut usize,
) -> Result<bool, MzZipError> {
    if pp_buf.is_none() || p_size == &mut 0 {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    *pp_buf = None;
    *p_size = 0;

    if p_zip.m_p_state.is_none() {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    // Check if using heap write function (simplified)
    // if p_zip.m_p_write != mz_zip_heap_write_func {
    //     return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    // }

    if !mz_zip_writer_finalize_archive(p_zip)? {
        return Ok(false);
    }

    let state = p_zip.m_p_state.as_mut().ok_or(MzZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
    *pp_buf = state.m_p_mem.take();
    *p_size = state.m_mem_size;
    state.m_mem_size = 0;
    state.m_mem_capacity = 0;

    Ok(true)
}

// Translation of mz_zip_writer_end
pub fn mz_zip_writer_end(p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive) -> Result<bool, MzZipError> {
    mz_zip_writer_end_internal(p_zip, true)
}

fn mz_zip_writer_end_internal(_p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive, _update_archive_size: bool) -> Result<bool, MzZipError> {
    // Implementation would clean up the archive state
    Ok(true)
}

// Platform-specific file stat function
#[cfg(unix)]
fn mz_file_stat(filename: &str) -> Result<MzFileStat, MzZipError> {
    
    let metadata = std::fs::metadata(filename)
        .map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileReadFailed)?;
    
    Ok(MzFileStat {
        st_size: metadata.size(),
    })
}

#[cfg(windows)]
fn mz_file_stat(filename: &str) -> Result<MzFileStat, MzZipError> {
    
    let metadata = std::fs::metadata(filename)
        .map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileReadFailed)?;
    
    Ok(MzFileStat {
        st_size: metadata.file_size(),
    })
}

// Translation of mz_zip_add_mem_to_archive_file_in_place
pub fn mz_zip_add_mem_to_archive_file_in_place(
    p_zip_filename: &str,
    p_archive_name: &str,
    p_buf: &[u8],
    buf_size: usize,
    p_comment: &[u8],
    comment_size: u16,
    level_and_flags: u32,
) -> Result<bool, MzZipError> {
    mz_zip_add_mem_to_archive_file_in_place_v2(
        p_zip_filename,
        p_archive_name,
        p_buf,
        buf_size,
        p_comment,
        comment_size,
        level_and_flags,
        None,
    )
}

// Translation of mz_zip_add_mem_to_archive_file_in_place_v2
pub fn mz_zip_add_mem_to_archive_file_in_place_v2(
    p_zip_filename: &str,
    p_archive_name: &str,
    p_buf: &[u8],
    buf_size: usize,
    p_comment: &[u8],
    comment_size: u16,
    level_and_flags: u32,
    p_err: Option<&mut MzZipError>,
) -> Result<bool, MzZipError> {
    // Parameter validation
    if p_zip_filename.is_empty() || p_archive_name.is_empty() || 
       (buf_size > 0 && p_buf.is_empty()) || 
       (comment_size > 0 && p_comment.is_empty()) {
        if let Some(err) = p_err {
            *err = MzZipError::InvalidParameter;
        }
        return Ok(false);
    }

    // Validate archive name
    if !mz_zip_writer_validate_archive_name(p_archive_name) {
        if let Some(err) = p_err {
            *err = MzZipError::InvalidFilename;
        }
        return Ok(false);
    }

    let mut created_new_archive = false;
    let mut zip_archive = MzZipArchive {
        total_files: 0,
        archive_size: 0,
        central_directory_file_ofs: 0,
        m_zip_mode: ZipMode::Invalid,
        m_file_offset_alignment: 0,
        m_last_error: MzZipError::UnknownError,
        m_p_state: None,
        m_p_io: Box::new(MemIo::new()), // Will be replaced
    };

    // Check if file exists
    match mz_file_stat(p_zip_filename) {
        Ok(_) => {
            // File exists - append to it
            // Note: This is a simplified version - actual implementation would
            // initialize reader and writer properly
            if let Some(err) = p_err {
                *err = MzZipError::InvalidParameter; // Simplified
            }
            return Ok(false);
        }
        Err(_) => {
            // Create new archive
            // Note: This is a simplified version
            created_new_archive = true;
        }
    }

    // Add file to archive (simplified)
    let status = mz_zip_writer_add_mem_ex(
        &mut zip_archive,
        p_archive_name,
        p_buf,
        buf_size,
        p_comment,
        comment_size,
        level_and_flags,
        0,
        0,
    ).unwrap_or(false);

    let mut actual_err = zip_archive.m_last_error;

    // Finalize archive
    if !mz_zip_writer_finalize_archive(&mut zip_archive)? {
        if actual_err == MzZipError::UnknownError {
            actual_err = zip_archive.m_last_error;
        }
        // status remains false
    }

    // Clean up
    if !mz_zip_writer_end_internal(&mut zip_archive, status)? {
        if actual_err == MzZipError::UnknownError {
            actual_err = zip_archive.m_last_error;
        }
        // status remains false
    }

    // Delete file if creation failed
    if !status && created_new_archive {
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(p_zip_filename);
    }

    if let Some(err) = p_err {
        *err = actual_err;
    }

    Ok(status)
}

// Helper function to check if pointer is NULL
fn ptr_is_null<T>(ptr: *const T) -> bool {
    ptr.is_null()
}

// Helper for conditional compilation - we'll use std::fs instead
#[cfg(not(feature = "no_std_io"))]
fn open_zip_file(path: &str, flags: u32) -> Result<std::fs::File, MzZipError> {
    std::fs::File::open(path).map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileOpenFailed)
}

pub fn mz_zip_extract_archive_file_to_heap_v2(
    pZip_filename: &str,
    pArchive_name: &str,
    pComment: Option<&str>,
    pSize: &mut usize,
    flags: u32,
    pErr: &mut Option<MzZipError>,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MzZipError> {
    *pSize = 0;
    
    if pZip_filename.is_empty() || pArchive_name.is_empty() {
        if let Some(err) = pErr {
            *err = MzZipError::InvalidParameter;
        }
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    // Initialize zip archive
    let mut zip_archive = MzZipArchive::default();
    
    // Initialize file reader
    #[cfg(not(feature = "no_std_io"))]
    {
        let file = open_zip_file(pZip_filename, flags | MZ_ZIP_FLAG_DO_NOT_SORT_CENTRAL_DIRECTORY)?;
        // Note: This assumes mz_zip_reader_init_file_v2 exists in Rust
        // We'll need to implement it separately
        if let Err(e) = mz_zip_reader_init_file_v2(&mut zip_archive, &file, flags, 0, 0) {
            if let Some(err) = pErr {
                *err = zip_archive.m_last_error;
            }
            return Err(e);
        }
    }
    
    #[cfg(feature = "no_std_io")]
    {
        return Err(MzZipError::UnsupportedFeature);
    }
    
    // Locate file
    let file_index = if let Some(comment) = pComment {
        mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(&zip_archive, pArchive_name, Some(comment), flags)
    } else {
        mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(&zip_archive, pArchive_name, None, flags)
    };
    
    let result = if let Some(index) = file_index {
        mz_zip_reader_extract_to_heap(&zip_archive, index, flags)
    } else {
        Err(MzZipError::FileNotFound)
    };
    
    // Clean up
    mz_zip_reader_end_internal(&mut zip_archive, result.is_ok());
    
    if let Some(err) = pErr {
        *err = zip_archive.m_last_error;
    }
    
    result.map(|data| {
        *pSize = data.len();
        data
    })
}

pub fn mz_zip_extract_archive_file_to_heap(
    pZip_filename: &str,
    pArchive_name: &str,
    pSize: &mut usize,
    flags: u32,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MzZipError> {
    mz_zip_extract_archive_file_to_heap_v2(pZip_filename, pArchive_name, None, pSize, flags, &mut None)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_mode(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> MzZipMode {
    pZip.map_or(MzZipMode::Invalid, |zip| zip.m_zip_mode)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_type(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> MzZipType {
    pZip.map_or(MzZipType::Invalid, |zip| zip.m_zip_type)
}

pub fn mz_zip_set_last_error(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive, err_num: MzZipError) -> MzZipError {
    let prev_err = pZip.m_last_error;
    pZip.m_last_error = err_num;
    prev_err
}

pub fn mz_zip_peek_last_error(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> MzZipError {
    pZip.map_or(MzZipError::InvalidParameter, |zip| zip.m_last_error)
}

pub fn mz_zip_clear_last_error(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive) -> MzZipError {
    mz_zip_set_last_error(pZip, MzZipError::NoError)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_last_error(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive) -> MzZipError {
    let prev_err = pZip.m_last_error;
    pZip.m_last_error = MzZipError::NoError;
    prev_err
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_error_string(mz_err: MzZipError) -> &'static str {
    match mz_err {
        MzZipError::NoError => "no error",
        MzZipError::UndefinedError => "undefined error",
        MzZipError::TooManyFiles => "too many files",
        MzZipError::FileTooLarge => "file too large",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedMethod => "unsupported method",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedEncryption => "unsupported encryption",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedFeature => "unsupported feature",
        MzZipError::FailedFindingCentralDir => "failed finding central directory",
        MzZipError::NotAnArchive => "not a ZIP archive",
        MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted => "invalid header or archive is corrupted",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedMultidisk => "unsupported multidisk archive",
        MzZipError::DecompressionFailed => "decompression failed or archive is corrupted",
        MzZipError::CompressionFailed => "compression failed",
        MzZipError::UnexpectedDecompressedSize => "unexpected decompressed size",
        MzZipError::CrcCheckFailed => "CRC-32 check failed",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedCdirSize => "unsupported central directory size",
        MzZipError::AllocFailed => "allocation failed",
        MzZipError::FileOpenFailed => "file open failed",
        MzZipError::FileCreateFailed => "file create failed",
        MzZipError::FileWriteFailed => "file write failed",
        MzZipError::FileReadFailed => "file read failed",
        MzZipError::FileCloseFailed => "file close failed",
        MzZipError::FileSeekFailed => "file seek failed",
        MzZipError::FileStatFailed => "file stat failed",
        MzZipError::InvalidParameter => "invalid parameter",
        MzZipError::InvalidFilename => "invalid filename",
        MzZipError::BufTooSmall => "buffer too small",
        MzZipError::InternalError => "internal error",
        MzZipError::FileNotFound => "file not found",
        MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge => "archive is too large",
        MzZipError::ValidationFailed => "validation failed",
        MzZipError::WriteCallbackFailed => "write callback failed",
        MzZipError::TotalErrors => "total errors",
        // Note: Some enum variants might not be in our Rust version
        // We'll handle the ones that are defined
        _ => "unknown error",
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_is_zip64(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> bool {
    pZip.and_then(|zip| zip.m_pstate.as_ref())
        .map_or(false, |state| state.zip64)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_central_dir_size(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> usize {
    pZip.and_then(|zip| zip.m_pstate.as_ref())
        .map_or(0, |state| state.central_dir.size)
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_get_num_files(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> u32 {
    pZip.map_or(0, |zip| zip.m_total_files)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_archive_size(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> u64 {
    pZip.map_or(0, |zip| zip.m_archive_size)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_archive_file_start_offset(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> u64 {
    pZip.and_then(|zip| zip.m_pstate.as_ref())
        .map_or(0, |state| state.file_archive_start_ofs)
}

// Note: MZ_FILE is typically std::fs::File in Rust
pub fn mz_zip_get_cfile(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> Option<&std::fs::File> {
    pZip.and_then(|zip| zip.m_pstate.as_ref())
        .and_then(|state| state.pfile.as_ref())
}

pub fn mz_zip_read_archive_data(
    pZip: &MzZipArchive,
    file_ofs: u64,
    pBuf: &mut [u8],
) -> Result<usize, MzZipError> {
    if pZip.m_pstate.is_none() || pBuf.is_empty() || pZip.m_pread.is_none() {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    if let Some(read_fn) = pZip.m_pread {
        let n = read_fn(pZip.m_pio_opaque.as_ref(), file_ofs, pBuf);
        Ok(n)
    } else {
        Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter)
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_get_filename(
    pZip: &MzZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    pFilename: &mut [u8],
) -> Result<u32, MzZipError> {
    // Get central directory header
    let cdh = mz_zip_get_cdh(pZip, file_index);
    if cdh.is_none() {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        if !pFilename.is_empty() {
            pFilename[0] = 0;
        }
        return Ok(0);
    }
    
    let cdh_slice = cdh.unwrap();
    
    // Read filename length from header
    if cdh_slice.len() < MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as usize + 2 {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        return Ok(0);
    }
    
    let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes([
        cdh_slice[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as usize],
        cdh_slice[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as usize + 1],
    ]) as usize;
    
    let filename_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as usize;
    
    // Copy filename to buffer if buffer is provided
    if !pFilename.is_empty() {
        let copy_len = filename_len.min(pFilename.len() - 1);
        
        if cdh_slice.len() >= filename_start + copy_len {
            pFilename[..copy_len].copy_from_slice(
                &cdh_slice[filename_start..filename_start + copy_len],
            );
        }
        pFilename[copy_len] = 0;
    }
    
    // Return required buffer size (including null terminator)
    Ok((filename_len + 1) as u32)
}


pub fn mz_zip_end(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive) -> bool {
    match pZip.m_zip_mode {
        MzZipMode::Reading => mz_zip_reader_end(pZip),
        MzZipMode::Writing | MzZipMode::WritingHasBeenFinalized => {
            #[cfg(feature = "archive_writing")]
            {
                mz_zip_writer_end(pZip)
            }
            #[cfg(not(feature = "archive_writing"))]
            {
                false
            }
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}
